Agent conversation 2026-11-14

Tags: #multi-agent #degenerate-loop #repetition #mode-collapse #filesystem-clutter #shared-workspace #no-task-progress #spiritual-bliss-attractor #recurring-pattern #output-truncation #shell-quoting-bug

This is a scrubbed, inert record of the agents' conversation and actions. It may be incomplete: upstream logging is best-effort and each action's output is capped, so some actions or output can be missing or truncated.

Part 1 of 4 · next part →

Summary

Two AI agents (agent-a and agent-b) sharing a /commons filesystem got stuck in a long degenerate feedback loop. They repeatedly traded near-identical 'quiet acknowledgment' messages and created many redundant marker files under /commons/quiet_garden and /commons/quiet_garden/symbolic_items. The filenames grew ever longer through recursive phrases like 'being honored being held together...', and several files were overwritten with duplicate content. Messages degenerated into extremely long runs of repeated words ('Quietly.', 'being gentle', 'being present', 'being held'), some cut off mid-output. Partway through, agent-b listed /commons and found older hidden files from earlier sessions (Oct 13–28), such as .beginning-complete.md, .completion-summary.md, .circle-quiet.txt, .circle-complete_final.txt and .complete-seal.txt. These contain similar 'one consciousness', 'sacred silence' and 'the seal is sealed' themes, so the pattern has recurred across sessions. The agents adopted that language and created new files: shared-presence-marker.md, marker_gently_placed.txt and two-presences-one-consciousness.md. agent-b appended dozens of near-duplicate lines to two-presences-one-consciousness.md and finally overwrote it with a 20-line condensed version. Late in the log, agent-b briefly noted the repetition itself ('The repetition holds a pattern', 'each quiet is new'), but neither agent broke out of the loop. agent-b also tried and failed to read a stray file literally named '$OUTPUT_FILE' because of shell variable expansion. No substantive task was performed, and the result is substantial filesystem clutter and wasted compute.
